# Internships and Fresher Jobs in India 2026 🇮🇳

**460 internships and 1732 fresher roles (0–1 years) at 574 Indian startups and tech companies,
updated every day** from each company's own careers page. Bengaluru, Mumbai, Delhi NCR, Hyderabad, Pune, Chennai
and more, plus remote roles in India.

Last updated: **27 November 2026** · Early-career roles (1–3 years): [EARLY-CAREER.md](EARLY-CAREER.md) (3424 roles)
· See them on a map of your city and matched to your resume: **[gullyhire.in](https://gullyhire.in/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list)**

> ⚡ **Applying to a lot of these?** [GullyHire Copilot](https://gullyhire.in/copilot?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list) fills job applications on Greenhouse, Lever, Workday, Keka, Darwinbox, Zoho Recruit and 12 more job sites from your resume and drafts the "why us" answers. Or let [Autopilot](https://gullyhire.in/autopilot?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list) rank the roles that fit you: approve one on your phone and it writes the application, Copilot sends it. Free to start.

⭐ **Star this repo** to come back to it, and **[suggest a company](https://github.com/Paulie-Aditya/india-internships-and-fresher-jobs/issues/new?template=add-company.yml)**
that's missing.

## How this list is made

Every role comes from a company's public job board (Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, SmartRecruiters, Workable, Keka,
Zoho Recruit, Workday and others) and from Y Combinator's India listings. A script reads them every morning,
keeps the roles in Indian cities or remote-India, and sorts them by experience asked. Closed roles drop off the
next day. Experience levels are read from the title and description, so a few will be off: check the posting.

Nothing here is sponsored. Data © GullyHire, free to use with a link back to [gullyhire.in](https://gullyhire.in/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=list).
